## Check D: Cross-Workflow Data Availability for T2-Future Annotations

**Question:** Does the test-skill workflow have access to enrichment data? How does it know whether T2-future annotations exist?

This is the most significant structural finding.

**What the new rule requires:**

> "If T2-future annotations exist in the enrichment data, verify that Section 4b is present..."

This requires the test-skill workflow to know whether any T2-future annotations were produced during create-skill's step-04-enrich. That enrichment data lives in the create-skill pipeline's in-context extraction inventory and, if written to disk, in the skill's `references/` files, `provenance-map.json`, and `evidence-report.md`.

**What test-skill actually has access to:**

Per workflow-plan-test-skill.md, step-01-init.md loads:
- `{skillsOutputFolder}/{skill_name}/SKILL.md`
- `{skillsOutputFolder}/{skill_name}/metadata.json`
- `{sidecar_path}/forge-tier.yaml`

The workflow's data flow (workflow-plan-test-skill.md lines 218-223) lists only those three sources as step-01 inputs. None of step-02 through step-06 list `provenance-map.json`, `evidence-report.md`, or any `references/` file as inputs.

**What create-skill actually produces:**

create-skill step-05-compile.md builds `provenance-map.json` (with T2 provenance citations) and `evidence-report.md` (with enrichment summary counts including T2-future annotation count). These are written to the staging directory and then promoted to the skill output folder by step-07. So `provenance-map.json` and `evidence-report.md` **are present** in the skill output directory alongside SKILL.md.

**The gap:**

The new rule's phrase "enrichment data" is ambiguous. The test-skill workflow has no explicit instruction to read `provenance-map.json` or `evidence-report.md` to determine whether T2-future annotations exist. The rule as written tells a step to check "the enrichment data" without telling any step where to find it.

One alternative reading is that the check is structural only: if Section 4b is present in SKILL.md, T2-future annotations must have existed (because create-skill only emits Section 4b when they do). This would make the check purely a SKILL.md structural inspection, not a cross-artifact data lookup. But the rule also says "each warning traces to a T2 provenance citation" — which requires reading `provenance-map.json` or the `references/` files, not just SKILL.md.

**RESULT: FAIL**

The new rule references "enrichment data" without specifying the artifact path, and no test-skill step is given instructions to load `provenance-map.json`, `evidence-report.md`, or `references/` files. The workflow-plan's data flow and context boundaries do not include these artifacts. As written the rule cannot be executed as specified because the executing step has no source to read T2-future annotation counts from.

**Required fix:** The rule (or the step that implements it, once Check A is resolved) must specify the exact artifact to read. Given what create-skill produces, the natural source is `evidence-report.md` (which contains "T2-future annotations: {count}" in its enrichment summary) or `provenance-map.json`. The step that implements this check must list that artifact in its "CONTEXT BOUNDARIES: Available" section. The workflow-plan data flow table should also be updated.

## Issues Requiring Action

### Issue 1 — FAIL (Confidence: 92)

**File:** `/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/src/workflows/create-skill/steps-c/step-03b-fetch-temporal.md`, lines 46 and 74 and 138

**Category C1 — Data Contract Mismatch**

`step-03b` declares it consumes `extraction_inventory.top_exports[]` as a named array field. Step-03 never creates a field by this name. Step-03 section 4 defines a flat list of per-export entries and a display-only "Top exports" summary in the Gate 2 message. No structured `top_exports` array is written into the inventory as a named key.

This means an agent following step-03 literally will not produce `extraction_inventory.top_exports[]`, and step-03b's fingerprint computation (section 2) and targeted searches (section 3b) have no well-defined input.

**Fix:** Either add an explicit `top_exports[]` field definition to step-03 section 4's inventory schema (e.g., as part of the aggregate counts: "top_exports: sorted list of the top 10-20 public API function names by prominence"), or restate step-03b's consumption in terms of the actual inventory structure (e.g., "iterate all per-export entries, filter to public API surface, sort by name").

---

### Issue 2 — FAIL (Confidence: 88)

**File:** `/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/src/workflows/create-skill/steps-c/step-05-compile.md`, lines 245–266 (success/failure metrics section)

**Category E — Success/Failure Metrics Missing New Behaviors**

The success and failure metrics were not updated to cover the two major new behaviors introduced in the body of this edited file:

1. **Section 4b (Migration & Deprecation Warnings):** The body (lines 117–122) adds a conditional Section 4b that must be emitted when T2-future annotations exist and must be entirely omitted (not left empty) when they don't. Neither the success metrics nor the failure metrics mention Section 4b at all.

2. **Two-tier assembly:** The body (lines 83–179) introduces a significant two-tier structure (Tier 1 under 300 lines, Tier 2 reference-eligible), assembly order, and the 300-line Tier 1 budget rule. The success metrics have no entry for two-tier assembly compliance, and the failure metrics have no entry for Tier 1 exceeding 300 lines or for Tier 2 sections appearing before Tier 1 sections.

**Fix:** Add to success metrics:
- "Section 4b emitted only when Deep tier AND T2-future annotations exist; omitted entirely otherwise (no empty section)"
- "Tier 1 sections assembled first and kept under 300 lines (excluding frontmatter)"
- "Tier 2 sections assembled after all Tier 1 sections"

Add to failure metrics:
- "Emitting an empty Section 4b when no T2-future annotations exist"
- "Tier 1 content exceeding 300 lines without reduction"
- "Assembling Tier 2 sections before Tier 1 sections"

### Issue 3 — NOTE (Confidence: 82)

**Files:** `/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/src/workflows/create-skill/validation-report.md` and `/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/src/workflows/create-skill/workflow-plan-create-skill.md`

**Category B — Stale Documentation**

The `validation-report.md` (written 2026-02-26) lists only 8 step files and does not include `step-03b-fetch-temporal.md`. It records the step chain as step-03 → step-04 (without 03b). This is not a blocking functional issue because the live step files implement the correct chain. However, if any automated validator reads these documents to verify the step count or chain structure, it will produce false results. The `validation-report.md` also incorrectly states "8 step files present" when there are now 9.

**Fix:** Update `validation-report.md` to add step-03b to the file structure table and the frontmatter validation table. Update the step sequence table in `workflow-plan-create-skill.md` to include step-03b between step-03 and step-04, and update the step count from 8 to 9.

+#### 3b. Targeted Function Searches (Uses Extraction Inventory)
+
+After the generic fetches above, perform **targeted searches** using the top-level public API function names from `extraction_inventory.top_exports[]`. This produces high-signal results that generic list fetches miss.
+
+**Limit:** Search the top **10 function names** maximum to control API call volume and avoid `gh` rate limiting.
+
+For each function name in `top_exports[]` (up to 10):
+
+```bash
+gh search issues --repo {owner}/{repo} "{function_name}" --limit 5 --json number,title,state,body
+```
+
+Aggregate all targeted search results into a single file: `{staging}/targeted-issues.md`. Format as a markdown document with one section per function name, listing the matching issues/PRs found.
+
+**If `gh search` is unavailable** (older `gh` CLI versions): skip targeted searches silently. The generic fetches from section 3 still provide baseline temporal context.
+
+**If rate limiting occurs** (HTTP 429 or similar): stop targeted searches immediately, keep results collected so far. Log: "Targeted search stopped at function {N}/{total} due to rate limiting."
+
+**After all fetching,** verify at least one file was written to the staging directory. If the staging directory is empty (all fetches failed), log a warning and skip to section 5.
